# Graph Report - gestion-clinique  (2026-11-17)

## Corpus Check
- 3433 files · ~2,893,579 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 236 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 116, .example 42, .lock 32)

## Summary
- 53931 nodes · 131207 edges · 1674 communities (1247 shown, 427 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 93% EXTRACTED · 7% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 9653 edges (avg confidence: 0.91)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `2b63a983`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
